kon_kizuna_number = {
	value = 0
	every_interest_group = {
		limit = {
			is_in_government = yes
			leader = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = tot_Kon_Yui_trait
					has_trait = tot_Kon_Mio_trait
					has_trait = tot_Kon_Tsumugi_trait
					has_trait = tot_Kon_Ritsu_trait
					has_trait = tot_Kon_Azusa_trait
				}
			}
		}
		add = 1 
	}
}
tot_band_limit_value = {
	value = 1
	add = modifier:country_tot_band_limit_add
	max = 10
}

tot_band_created_count = {
	value = 0
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_2_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_3_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_4_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_5_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_6_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_7_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_8_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_9_created } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_10_created } add = 1 }
}

band_kizuna_number = {
	value = 0
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 1
	} 
}
band_kizuna_exp_progress = {
	value = 5
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
					has_trait = tot_drummer_trait
				}
				AND = {
					has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
					has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait
				}
				AND = {
					has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
					has_trait = tot_bassist_trait
				}
				AND = {
					has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
					has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait
				}
				AND = {
					has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
					has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait
				}
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait
		}
		add = 10
	}
}

band_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_kizuna_resource_weekly_gain_value = {
	value = 0
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_musical_instrument var:tot_band_invest_musical_instrument > 0 market.mg:musical_instrument = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_paper var:tot_band_invest_paper > 0 market.mg:paper = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_luxury_clothes var:tot_band_invest_luxury_clothes > 0 market.mg:luxury_clothes = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_transportation var:tot_band_invest_transportation > 0 market.mg:transportation = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_automobiles var:tot_band_invest_automobiles > 0 market.mg:automobiles = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_radios var:tot_band_invest_radios > 0 market.mg:radios = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_fruit var:tot_band_invest_fruit > 0 market.mg:fruit = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_tea var:tot_band_invest_tea > 0 market.mg:tea = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_coffee var:tot_band_invest_coffee > 0 market.mg:coffee = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_groceries var:tot_band_invest_groceries > 0 market.mg:groceries = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_luxury_drinks var:tot_band_invest_luxury_drinks > 0 market.mg:luxury_drinks = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_luxury_foods var:tot_band_invest_luxury_foods > 0 market.mg:luxury_foods = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 5 }
}

	value = 0
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_musical_instrument var:tot_band_invest_musical_instrument > 0 market.mg:musical_instrument = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_invest_paper var:tot_band_invest_paper > 0 market.mg:paper = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } } add = 1 }

band_kizuna_resource_multiplier_percent_value = {
	value = 100
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 2 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 3 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 4 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 5 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 6 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 7 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 8 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 9 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 10 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 11 } add = 10 }
	if = { limit = { band_kizuna_resource_variety_count_value >= 12 } add = 10 }
}

band_kizuna_creation_weekly_gain_value = {
	value = band_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value
	add = band_kizuna_resource_weekly_gain_value
	multiply = band_kizuna_resource_multiplier_percent_value
	divide = 100
}

band_mode_state_sponsored_bonus_value = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_band_headquarter
				has_active_production_method = pm_band_mode_state_sponsored
			}
		}
		add = 5
	}
}

band_mode_independent_bonus_value = {

		limit = {

				is_building_type = building_band_headquarter
				has_active_production_method = pm_band_mode_independent
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
}

tot_kizuna_001_number = {
	value = 0
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = tot_group_sheet_001_trait
			OR = {
				is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				is_heir_of_own_country = yes
				AND = {
					is_interest_group_leader = yes
					interest_group ?= { is_in_government = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		add = 1
	}
}

tot_gourmet_progress_bar_value = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_progress }
		add = var:tot_gourmet_progress
	}
	divide = 36000
	min = 0
	max = 1
}

tot_gourmet_variety_count_value = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_grain var:tot_gourmet_invest_grain > 0 }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_meat var:tot_gourmet_invest_meat > 0 }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_fish var:tot_gourmet_invest_fish > 0 }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_fruit var:tot_gourmet_invest_fruit > 0 }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_groceries var:tot_gourmet_invest_groceries > 0 }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_tea }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_coffee }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_luxury_drinks }
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_luxury_foods }
		add = 1
	}
}

tot_gourmet_weekly_gain_value = {
	value = tot_gourmet_base_weekly_gain_value

	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_grain market.mg:grain = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_meat market.mg:meat = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_fish market.mg:fish = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_fruit market.mg:fruit = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_groceries market.mg:groceries = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_tea market.mg:tea = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_coffee market.mg:coffee = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_luxury_drinks market.mg:luxury_drinks = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_luxury_foods market.mg:luxury_foods = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		add = 5
	}

	add = tot_gourmet_variety_bonus_value

	multiply = tot_gourmet_government_multiplier_percent_value
	divide = 100
}

band_1_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 1
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 1
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 1 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_2_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 2
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 2
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 2 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_3_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 3
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 3
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 3 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_4_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 4
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 4
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 4 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_5_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 5
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 5
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 5 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_6_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 6
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 6
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }

band_7_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 7
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 7
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 7 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_8_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 8
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 8
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 8 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_9_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 9
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 9
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 9 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}

band_10_kizuna_creation_base_gain_value = {
	value = 10
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 10
			OR = {
				has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
				has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
				has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
			}
		}
		add = 3
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = 10
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer has_trait = tot_drummer_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist has_trait = tot_guitarist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist has_trait = tot_bassist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist has_trait = tot_keyboardist_trait }
				AND = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait }
			}
		}
		add = 2
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 10 has_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		add = 10
	}
}
